# OpenText build pack

Everything an AI coding agent needs to rebuild the OpenText app UI in the Mesh Gradient style for SwiftUI (iOS): the brief, the design tokens, the real code that draws the UI kit and screens and the logo, with a prompt at the top that says how to use it. Generated by Looks Good.

## Part 0: How to use this file (prompt for the agent)

This section is addressed to you, the AI coding agent. Read it first and follow it exactly. It tells you what this file is, which part wins when two disagree, what to do in what order, and what to report when you finish.

**What this file is.** The build pack for OpenText: App UI, Mesh Gradient style, for SwiftUI (iOS). It holds the brand brief (Part 1), the design tokens (Part 2), the real CSS and reference markup that draw every UI kit component (Part 3) and every screen layout (Part 4), the logo (Part 6) and a checklist you must pass (Part 7). Everything you need is here; do not redraw anything from memory and do not invent values this file does not give.

**Authority order.** When two things in this file disagree, the earlier one wins:

1. Part 1 brief rules: what is allowed, where, and why.
2. Part 2 tokens: the only colours, type faces and spacing values you may use.
3. Part 3 component CSS values (`kit.css`): every colour, size, radius, border, shadow and type setting of every component.
4. Part 3 and Part 4 reference markup: illustrative. It shows structure and intent; class names are Looks Good's own, and the demo copy ("Brand refresh", "Maya Ortiz") is placeholder text.

**Steps.**

1. Save this file in the project root as `BUILD-PACK.md`, and read Part 1 completely before writing code.
2. Add `Theme.swift` from Part 2 to the app target. Every colour, font and shadow in the project comes from it.
3. Build each UI kit component from Part 3. `kit.css` is a specification of values (CSS pixels are points): read each component's colours, sizes, radii, borders, shadows, type and every state from it and draw it in SwiftUI with those values and the Theme.swift tokens.
4. For SwiftUI, Part 3 marks each component Native or Custom. Native rows: use the system control (`Toggle`, `Picker`, `.searchable` and so on), tinted with Theme.swift tokens only, and let the system supply its behaviour. Custom rows: draw them yourself from the Part 3 CSS values.
5. Assemble screens from Part 4: each layout names its blocks in order, its rules, and gives the reference markup of the phone screen. Spacing, the 44pt tap target and the type scale come from Part 1 and do not change per screen.
6. Patterns are out of scope for this build: it is an App, and app screens carry no patterns or art.
7. Place the logo from Part 6 at the sizes and positions Part 1 gives.
8. Finish with the Part 7 checklist: go through every item, fix what fails, and do not call the work done until all of it passes.

**Hard rules** (they repeat Part 1 on purpose).

- No colour outside the tokens. No gradient, opacity or tint that is not derived from them as Part 1 says.
- No art and no patterns in app UI: app screens are built from the UI kit only.
- The demo copy in the reference markup ("Brand refresh", "Maya Ortiz" and the like) is placeholder: replace it with the product's real text, but keep the structure and length discipline.
- Keep the 44pt minimum tap target and the phone type scale (sizes, line heights, weights) exactly as Part 1 gives them.
- Match both light and dark exactly as the tokens define them; the dark palette is designed, not inverted.

**Report back** when you finish: (1) the files you created or changed; (2) which Part 7 items pass, and for any that do not, why; (3) every place you could not match a Part 3 value or a Part 1 rule, and what you used instead; (4) any placeholder copy you left in.

## Part 1: Brand brief

The full AI brief for this brand, unchanged apart from its headings being one step deeper. It holds the rules: colours and where each is used, type, shape, states, motion, accessibility and what is allowed where. Where anything in this file conflicts with it, the brief wins.

### OpenText — build brief
Build the real product from this brief, not a mock-up. Sections are marked Locked, Guided or Free. Locked values are final: use only these colors, fonts, shapes and motion, and where one is missing use the nearest listed token. Guided sections give an intent and a range; stay inside them. Free means design it for this product: layout, screen structure and how many sections a screen has. Screens are composed from the UI kit below, one component for each need; do not invent new component styles, and do not copy the brand board's layout. Target: SwiftUI (iOS 17 and macOS 14 or later).
OpenText: Fuck Preview. Aesthetic: Mesh Gradient (Fluid · Optimistic · Precise; Stripe / Fintech marketing / Gradient mesh). Mesh Gradient's look, carried through the product's controls, surfaces and type. Fluid on top, exact underneath.

#### App UI rules (Guided)
Build every screen from the UI kit only. The brand shows up through colour, type, corners, edges, shadow and how each component is drawn. Never put the board's hero illustration, deck art, patterns, textures, decorative bands or header art into app screens. The logo appears at icon size in the top bar or on the welcome screen, nowhere else. Images appear only when they are content (a user photo, a product photo). Never introduce a colour that is not in the palette: destructive and status colours come from it too.
The brand board, deck and landing page show art for the brand's identity and marketing; none of it belongs in the app.
Screens are composed from the UI kit and the phone layout below: pick the component for the need, set text in the type scale, and leave the rest to the tokens.
Focus rings appear on keyboard focus only (`:focus-visible` on the web, the system focus on SwiftUI), never on tap or click; disabled controls take no focus and no press response.

##### How Mesh Gradient shows up in the app
- Corners: buttons pill, fields 12pt, cards 20pt, badges pill.
- Surfaces: card --card, soft shadow; list rows --card, soft shadow; bottom sheet --card, soft shadow.
- Type: welcome title Plus Jakarta Sans (display) 300, tracking -0.01em; section labels Inter (body) 600, uppercase, tracking +0.1em; button labels Inter (body) 600, tracking -0.01em.
- Primary and selection: the primary button is a gradient from --primary to --ab-m-hue (at 135°) with a --on-primary label; the selected segment is a gradient from --primary to --ab-m-hue (at 140°); the selected tab shows a 22x4pt a gradient from --primary to --ab-m-hue (at 140°) pill, near the bottom of the box, centred.
- Signature details: a top bar filled --card; toggle: a 24pt --card dot, vertically centred in the box, at the left; pie menu: a 152pt --ink at 3% dot with a 1pt solid --line edge, vertically centred in the box, centred; skeleton screen: a a smooth fade of --canvas at 55% at 90° to transparent (a soft gloss, no hard line) sheet covering the whole box.

#### Why this works (Guided)
The reasons behind the style, most important first. Keep the reason and change the drawing where a board element does not fit a phone screen.

1. **Keep surfaces white; put colour in a few actions.** On the board: Luminous colour sits in a band beside the work. Headlines sit on white in dark ink, and colour never goes behind text. In the app: Gradient only on the primary button (135deg, two stops), progress bars, chart fills and the avatar. Canvas, cards and text stay opaque `Palette.ink` on white. No gradient behind body text or cards.
2. **Make one oversized title or numeral the hero.** On the board: A huge bold headline with a thick accent swash under one word, over small tracked labels. In the app: Large title 34–48pt or hero numeral 64–84pt in `Palette.ink`, at least 2x the next size. Labels 11–12pt uppercase, tracked. One highlighted word per screen, none on a screen without a title.
3. **Layer opaque cards with soft neutral shadows.** On the board: White cards with a 1px hairline, radius 12 and layered navy-grey shadows. Nothing translucent. In the app: Cards sit on the canvas with a 1px `Palette.line` hairline and a two-layer neutral shadow. List rows inside a card get no shadow of their own. At most two card levels deep.
4. **Spend space before adding sections.** On the board: Wide gaps between sections, and no space is filled with pattern. In the app: Section gaps 28–36pt, card padding 16–20pt. A Home screen with three blocks beats one with five. Cut a block before shrinking a gap.

Literal traps — board elements that do not reach the app as drawn:
- The striated band behind headers and hero: Leave it out. A screen header is plain canvas with the title in `Palette.ink`.
- The thick swash under every headline: One highlighted word per screen at most, and none on screens without a title.
- Numbered eyebrows like 01 / INTERFACE: Plain tracked labels with no numbers, especially on a screen with only three blocks.
- The soft mesh glow behind the page title and the product card on the band edge: Leave both out. The title sits on flat canvas.

Where this aesthetic's colour rules and a component description disagree, the colour rules win.
Palette colours no app component draws (brand board and app icon only; they stay in `:root`): `--secondary` #e0d1d1, `--accent-ink` #1c2126 / #cacd7e, `--info-icon` #73859a / #98b0cc, `--kit-stepper` #3b3a38.
- Primary budget: `Palette.primary` appears only where the UI kit draws it (Primary button, Text field, Search field, Checkbox, Radio, Toggle, Segmented control, List rows, Bottom sheet, Divider and section label, Tab bar, Badge and tag, Progress, Empty state, Avatar, Split button, Floating button, Link, Combo box, Listbox, Knob, Date picker, Calendar picker, Wheel picker, Wheel-style date picker, Carousel, Navigation bar, Anchor links, Scrollbar, Menu bar, Pie menu, Spinner), never as extra fills, text or rules. Selection, toggles, tabs and focus are drawn as their kit rows say, not in extra primary.
- Highlight word: use the brand's tagline unchanged as the welcome title (never reword it) and highlight its last word together with any full stop, wrapped in one span with `white-space: nowrap`; draw it as the Built Welcome screen does: a Palette.accent band 0.16em tall, full width, low across the word, its top edge 92% of the way down the line box (an underline band, not a full block).
- Text colours are the ones each UI kit row states; no other colours, tints or gradients on text.

#### UI kit (Locked)
Measured from the UI kit tab as Mesh Gradient draws it. Every control and container in the product is one of these components, drawn exactly as stated. Colours are tokens from Colors (a plain hex or rgba() is a fixed value the kit draws); "`--x` at N%" means `color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) N%, transparent)` and "at N% over `--canvas`" means `color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) N%, var(--canvas))`; sizes are points at the 390 x 844 reference (1pt = 1 CSS px); padding is CSS shorthand; exact shadow values are the `--shadow-*` tokens; (display) and (body) name the brand faces. "Other states" lists only what changes from the first state.
Native rows: use the system control and only tint it (accent, track, label font); keep its size, motion and behaviour. Custom rows: draw them exactly as described.

##### Actions

| Contextual menu | Actions for one item, on long press or right click. | Native `.contextMenu`, tinted | Palette.card | Palette.ink, Inter (body) 14pt 400 | no edge; 16pt corners; a soft Palette.kitPrimary shadow 2pt down, 4pt blur plus a soft Palette.kitSelect shadow 18pt down, 40pt blur | fits its content | chosen option: fill a smooth fade of Palette.priTint at 90° to transparent (a soft gloss, no hard line), Palette.ink, Inter (body) 17pt 600, 10pt corners; target row: fill a smooth fade of Palette.priTint at 90° to transparent (a soft gloss, no hard line), 58pt high, padding 8pt 16pt | one state |
| Submenu | A menu item that opens a second list of its own. | Native `Menu (nested)`, tinted | Palette.card | Palette.ink, Inter (body) 14pt 400 | no edge; 16pt corners; a soft Palette.kitPrimary shadow 2pt down, 4pt blur plus a soft Palette.kitSelect shadow 18pt down, 40pt blur | fits its content | open item: fill a smooth fade of Palette.priTint at 90° to transparent (a soft gloss, no hard line), 10pt corners, 44pt high; submenu: fill Palette.card, 16pt corners, a soft Palette.kitPrimary shadow 2pt down, 4pt blur plus a soft Palette.kitSelect shadow 18pt down, 40pt blur | one state |
| Expandable menu | Navigation items that open in place to show their children. | Native `OutlineGroup`, tinted | Palette.card | Palette.ink, Inter (body) 14pt 400 | no edge; 20pt corners; a soft Palette.kitPrimary shadow 1pt down, 2pt blur plus a soft Palette.kitPrimary-2 shadow 6pt down, 18pt blur | fits its content | parent row: 52pt high, padding 8pt 16pt; selected row: fill a smooth fade of Palette.priTint at 90° to transparent (a soft gloss, no hard line), Palette.ink, Inter (body) 17pt 600, 8pt corners, 52pt high, padding 8pt 16pt 8pt 62pt; child row: edge top 1pt solid Palette.abHair, 8pt corners, 52pt high, padding 8pt 16pt 8pt 62pt | one state |
| Pie menu | Up to 6 actions in a ring around the press point. | Custom | none | Palette.ink, Inter (body) 14pt 400 | no edge; square corners | fits its content | a 152pt Palette.ink at 3% dot with a 1pt solid Palette.line edge, vertically centred in the box, centred; hub: fill a gradient from Palette.primary to Palette.abMHue (at 140°), circle, a soft Palette.kitPrimary shadow 1pt down, 2pt blur plus a soft Palette.kitSecondary shadow 3pt down, 10pt blur, 14pt high; item: fill Palette.card, circle, a recessed inner Palette.kitPrimary-4 shadow (2pt down, 6pt blur), 44pt high, shifted -22,-22pt; chosen item: fill Palette.card, Palette.kitPrimary-5, Inter (body) 13pt 600, tracking -0.01em, circle, a recessed inner Palette.kitPrimary-4 shadow (2pt down, 6pt blur), 44pt high, shifted -22,-22pt | one state |

| Element | Use | iOS | Fill | Text | Edge, corners, shadow | Size | Parts and marks | Other states |

#### Screen layouts (Free)

##### Onboarding
As the Built Welcome screen (see Built screens).

##### List
A home or index screen: many items, one tap to open.

1. Top bar
2. Large title
3. Search field
4. Segmented control
5. Section label
6. List rows
7. Tab bar

- Large title that collapses into the top bar on scroll.
- Search and filters sit above the first section, never inside rows.
- Rows are 52pt minimum with one line of title and one of detail; trailing value or chevron.
- The list scrolls under the tab bar and ends above it; nothing hides behind it.
- Show at least 6 rows so the screen reads as a real list.

##### Detail
One item: its facts and what you can do with it. Top to bottom: Top bar, Large title, Badge and tag, Card, Section label, List rows, Primary button. One primary action, at the bottom or in the top bar, never both.

##### Form
Create or edit something. Top to bottom: Top bar, Large title, Text field, Text field, Select, Toggle, Callout, Primary button. Keyboard never covers the focused field or the primary action.

##### Settings
As the Settings composition under Built screens.

These five are starting points, not a fixed list: design each screen for its job from the kit's components, as one of these or a combination, with no hero areas or decorative headers.

#### Built screens (Guided)
A sample composition: the Mobile app tab of the Built view, measured at 390 x 844 and listed top to bottom; every y is in pt from the top of the screen. It shows how this style builds a phone screen. Match its components, type ladder and spacing rhythm, not the sample app's content, screen list or exact positions; the product's own screens are designed for its jobs. Components are drawn as their UI kit rows. Where this composition differs from Screen layouts (for example Home shows 3 list rows), it shows the style's intended density.
The Primary button's trailing mark (see its kit row) appears on every Primary button (Welcome and Home); other buttons have none.

##### Welcome
1. Status bar (pinned, the top 54pt): the time "9:41" at the left in --ink, system UI face 17pt 600; at the right signal bars (four rising bars, 18x11pt), Wi-Fi (three arcs, 16x11pt) and the battery (a rounded outline with its charge level and a small cap, 26x13pt) in --ink. The time starts 46pt from the left edge and the icons end 30pt from the right edge. A Dynamic Island is drawn: a 124x36pt black pill centred 11pt from the top (a device mock; leave it to the system).
2. Top band (pinned at 54pt from the top of the screen, 44pt high), no band fill (on the canvas); inside it: a 28x28pt logo chip (--ink, 9pt corners, a soft --kit-logo shadow 1pt down, 1pt blur plus a soft --kit-logo-2 shadow 4pt down, 10pt blur) holding the initial "O" in --canvas, Plus Jakarta Sans (display) 14pt 600, tracking -0.04em, then the name "OpenText" in --ink, Plus Jakarta Sans (display) 17pt 600, tracking -0.03em, and a "Skip" text button at the right margin (--primary-text, Inter (body) 17pt 600, tracking -0.01em).
3. The content block (eyebrow to feature rows) is centred vertically between the band and the pager; it starts with the eyebrow "Welcome" in --ink, Inter (body) 13pt 600, tracking +0.01em, with a 18x2pt --primary pill, before the text.
4. 10pt below the previous block: the welcome title, the brand's tagline word for word, in --ink, Plus Jakarta Sans (display) 34pt 300, tracking -0.01em on a 41pt line. It fits on one line; set `text-wrap: balance` so a longer tagline never leaves one word alone on a line.  Its last word carries the highlight (see Colors); the full stop sits just outside the coloured part, inside the same nowrap span.
5. 12pt below the previous block: one sentence of body in --ab-muted, Inter (body) 17pt 400, 2 lines at most (about 71 characters here).
6. 28pt below the previous block: 3 feature rows, 52pt each. Each starts with a 32x32pt icon chip (--pri-tint, 10pt corners) holding a 18pt icon in --kit-feature, then the title in --ink, Inter (body) 17pt 500. One grouped card holding all rows, separated by a 1pt solid --ab-hair rule, starting 62pt in from the left (under the text, not the lead).
7. Pinned at the bottom, from the top of the screen: 3 pager dots centred at 655pt (the current one a 20x7pt --primary-text pill, the others 7x7pt --ink at 55% over --canvas, 8pt apart), then the Primary button "Get started" at 686pt (label left-aligned, 18pt in, its trailing mark 9pt in from the right edge), then the Secondary button "I have an account" at 750pt; the last one is 52pt tall and ends 42pt above the bottom edge.
8. Home indicator (pinned): a 134x5pt --ink pill, centred, 8pt above the bottom edge.
Sample copy (keep the structure and length, write your app's content): feature rows: "One clear plan" (check icon), "Built for small teams" (bell icon), "Calm by design" (heart icon); eyebrow "Welcome"; body "Projects, tasks and the people doing them, organised in one calm place."; buttons "Get started" and "I have an account".

##### Home
1. Status bar (pinned, the top 54pt): the time "9:41" at the left in --ink, system UI face 17pt 600; at the right signal, Wi-Fi and battery in --ink. The time starts 46pt from the left edge and the icons end 30pt from the right edge. A Dynamic Island is drawn: a 124x36pt black pill centred 11pt from the top (a device mock; leave it to the system).
2. Top band (pinned at 54pt from the top of the screen, 75pt high), no band fill (on the canvas); inside it: the eyebrow "Today" in --ink, Inter (body) 13pt 600, tracking +0.01em, with a 18x2pt --primary pill, before the text; 4pt below it the screen title "Hi, Priya" in --ink, Plus Jakarta Sans (display) 34pt 600, tracking -0.01em; at the right margin, the notifications button: a 44x44pt button (--card, a soft --kit-primary shadow 1pt down, 2pt blur plus a soft --kit-secondary shadow 3pt down, 10pt blur) holding a 20pt bell icon in --ink, with a 8pt --accent status dot, a 2pt --canvas ring outside the edge on its top-right corner.
3. 20pt below the band: the summary Card, full width, 234pt tall. Inside, as y offsets from the card's top edge (these replace the kit card's padding): the Segmented control at 18pt (54pt tall; segments 149pt wide with padding 8pt 6pt; selected: text --kit-segmented-4 on a gradient from --primary to --ab-m-hue (at 140°); unselected: text --ab-muted on no fill), the card title "Launch site" at 88pt (28pt, --ink, Plus Jakarta Sans (display) 22pt 600, tracking -0.03em), the body at 122pt (20pt, --ab-muted, Inter (body) 15pt 400), the Primary button "Open project" at 162pt (52pt, label left-aligned, 18pt in, trailing mark 9pt in from the right edge); 20pt below the button to the card's bottom edge.
4. 28pt below the card: the section label "Up next" in --ink, Inter (body) 13pt 600, uppercase, tracking +0.1em, with a "See all" text button at the right margin (--primary-text, Inter (body) 15pt 600, tracking -0.01em).
5. 8pt below the previous block: the list, 3 rows of 58pt: the avatar, the title in --ink, Inter (body) 17pt 500 (sentence case, as written), a detail line in --ink at 75% over --canvas, Inter (body) 13pt 400, and the value with a 16pt chevron at the right in --ink at 75% over --canvas, Inter (body) 15pt 400. One grouped card holding all rows, separated by a 1pt solid --ab-hair rule, starting 62pt in from the left (under the text, not the lead). Avatars show one initial ("B", "U", "S"). Home shows exactly 3 rows, as the Built view does; the list ends 134pt above the tab bar and that space stays empty.
6. Tab bar (pinned at 761pt from the top of the screen, 83pt including the home indicator): --canvas at 92%, a top 1pt solid --line rule, 6pt side padding; 4 tabs, each a 24pt icon over its label, tab cells transparent. Per tab, as the Home screen draws it: "Home" (selected): home icon in --primary-text, label --ink; "Projects": list icon in --ink at 75% over --canvas, label --ink at 75% over --canvas; "Inbox": heart icon in --ink at 75% over --canvas, label --ink at 75% over --canvas; "Profile": user icon in --ink at 75% over --canvas, label --ink at 75% over --canvas. The selected label has no chip, with a 22x4pt a gradient from --primary to --ab-m-hue (at 140°) pill, near the bottom of the box, centred. Labels are text, never solid blocks of the label colour. On Settings the selected tab is "Profile" (Settings lives under it).
7. Home indicator (pinned): a 134x5pt --ink pill, centred, 8pt above the bottom edge.
Sample copy (keep the structure and length, write your app's content): segments "Mine", "Team"; card "Launch site", "Two tasks due before Friday", button "Open project"; rows "Brand refresh" / "Review" / "72%"; "User interviews" / "Writing" / "88%"; "Spring campaign" / "Planning" / "10%"; section "Up next", "See all".

##### Settings (compose from the kit)
1. Status bar 54pt, then the Top bar (pinned) with only the back button (a bare 22pt chevron icon in --ink, 44x44pt hit area).
2. Below the top bar, one scroll container (`flex: 1; overflow-y: auto`) from the top bar's bottom (98pt from the top of the screen) to the tab bar's top (761pt). Inside it, flowing: the Large title "Settings" 8pt down; 16pt below it the first group, a section label (18pt) with its list 8pt below it, 3 rows of 52pt (account rows: label, value and chevron); 28pt below it the second group, 2 rows, the last one a label with a Toggle; then 28pt below it the Destructive button ("Sign out", 52pt) alone. That totals 485pt of the 663pt between the bars, so "Sign out" is visible at rest; more groups (About, Help) go below it only if they still leave it on screen, otherwise above it with the screen scrolling.
3. Every group has its section label. Lists group as the Home list does. Rows without a lead start their divider 16pt in from the left edge of the list. The Toggle row is shown on.
4. The tab bar stays at the bottom (Settings is a tab); nothing pinned overlaps it.

#### Phone layout and type (Guided)
| Role | Face | Size pt | Line | Weight | SwiftUI style |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Display | Plus Jakarta Sans (`PlusJakartaSans-Light`) (display), tracking -0.01em | 34 | 41 | 300 | .largeTitle |
| Eyebrow | Inter (`Inter-SemiBold`) (body), tracking +0.01em | 13 | 18 | 600 | .caption |
| Label | Inter (`Inter-SemiBold`) (body), uppercase, tracking +0.1em | 13 | 18 | 600 | .caption |
| Large title | Plus Jakarta Sans (`PlusJakartaSans-SemiBold`) (display), tracking -0.01em | 34 | 41 | 600 | .largeTitle |
| Title | Plus Jakarta Sans (`PlusJakartaSans-SemiBold`) (display), tracking -0.01em | 28 | 34 | 600 | .title |
| Title 2 | Plus Jakarta Sans (`PlusJakartaSans-SemiBold`) (display), tracking -0.01em | 22 | 28 | 600 | .title2 |
| Headline | Inter (`Inter-Medium`) (body) | 17 | 22 | 500 | .headline |
| Body | Inter (`Inter-Regular`) (body) | 17 | 24 | 400 | .body |
| Callout | Inter (`Inter-Regular`) (body) | 16 | 22 | 400 | .callout |
| Subhead | Inter (`Inter-Regular`) (body) | 15 | 20 | 400 | .subheadline |
| Footnote | Inter (`Inter-Regular`) (body) | 13 | 18 | 400 | .footnote |
| Caption | Inter (`Inter-SemiBold`) (body) | 12 | 16 | 600 | .caption |
| Tab label | Inter (`Inter-SemiBold`) (body), tracking -0.01em | 10 | 12 | 600 selected, 500 unselected | .caption2 |
- Each role is a `Font.brand<Role>` in Theme.swift (for example `Font.brandLargeTitle`), a custom font that scales with Dynamic Type through its text style.
- Phone chrome: the status bar takes 54pt at the top and the home indicator 34pt at the bottom; nothing tappable sits in either. The top bar is 44pt high, under the status bar. At rest it shows only the back button and icon actions, with the Large title below it in the content; the title appears in the top bar (Headline size) only once the Large title has scrolled under it.
- Title colours: welcome title --ink, screen titles --ink, eyebrows --ink.
- Layout: 20pt side margins on every screen. Spacing comes from 4, 8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 28, 32, 40, 48pt only; 28pt between sections. Sizes: buttons 52pt, fields 48pt, list rows 52pt with one line and 58pt with a detail line, card insides as the Home card in Built screens, tab bar 83pt, nothing tappable under 44pt.
- Support Dynamic Type; never let a word split or a label truncate; one Large title per screen.

#### Shape and materials (Locked)
Corners, edges and shadows are the UI kit rows above; use `style: .continuous` on SwiftUI rounded shapes.
CSS to SwiftUI: a box-shadow blur B is `.shadow(radius: B/2)`; `inset` shadows use the `Recessed` modifier in Theme.swift; `linear-gradient(135deg, ...)` is `LinearGradient(startPoint: .topLeading, endPoint: .bottomTrailing)`; `rgba()` is `Color(.sRGB, red:, green:, blue:, opacity:)`; `backdrop-filter` is a SwiftUI `Material`.

#### Light and dark (Locked)
The default look is light, as the Built screens draw it. Dark is used only when the system asks for it: adaptive colours from Theme.swift follow the system appearance. The dark values are designed, not inverted. Text on the canvas uses `--ink` / `--muted`; text on cards and paper uses `--card-ink` / `--card-muted`, with each mode's value from the Colors table.

#### States (Locked)
| State | Colour | Visual |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Pressed and disabled | as each UI kit row states | The "Other states" column of the UI kit is the one definition; nothing else changes on press. Disabled controls take no press response. |
| Focus | `Palette.primary` where it holds 3:1 on the canvas, else `Palette.ink` | Keyboard focus on any control: a 2pt ring with a 2pt gap, following the control's corners; exactly as the "focus" state of the Primary and Secondary button rows in the UI kit draws it. Shown on keyboard focus only (`:focus-visible` on the web), never on tap or click; never removed. Text fields show their own focus state from the UI kit instead. |
| Empty | `Palette.ink` headline, `Palette.muted` copy | The Empty state row of the UI kit. |
| Loading | `Palette.line` track, `Palette.primary` fill | Indeterminate bar, or skeleton blocks in `Palette.line` easing between 100% and 60% opacity every 1.2 s. Show it after 300 ms of waiting, with a text label for screen readers. |
| Error | `Palette.error` | The Text field row's error state, with the message under the field. |

#### Motion (Guided)
- Durations: fast 120 ms (press, toggle), base 180 ms (color, selection, shadow), slow 280 ms (sheets, page changes). Easing: ease-out (`Motion.press`, `Motion.settle` in Theme.swift).
- Reduce Motion: Replace slides and fades with an instant state swap; colour changes stay. State changes stay; only the movement goes. Nothing else animates idly or loops except loading indicators.

#### Accessibility (Locked)
- Contrast kept (light | dark): ink on canvas 15.1 | 15.1 (AAA); muted on canvas 4.9 | 6.3 (AA); on-primary on primary 8.3 | 8.3 (AAA); on-accent on accent 11.0 | 11.0 (AAA); error on canvas 15.1 | 15.1 (AAA). Keep every text pair at 4.5:1 or better (3:1 for the accent-ink highlight word); do not swap tokens in a way that breaks them.
- Every tappable target is at least 44x44pt even when the visible shape is smaller, with 8pt between neighbours. Text scales: Dynamic Type through the `Font.brand*` styles, `@ScaledMetric` for icon sizes and paddings; layouts reflow at the largest accessibility size (stack, wrap, never truncate a primary label).
- Reduce Transparency (`accessibilityReduceTransparency`): translucent fills in the kit (tab bar, progress, overlay) become opaque. Increase Contrast (`colorSchemeContrast == .increased`): Hairlines become 2pt `Palette.ink`, `Palette.muted` text becomes `Palette.ink`, tints get a 1pt `Palette.ink` edge.
- Reduce Motion (`accessibilityReduceMotion`): see Motion. Never color alone: selection, status and errors also use an icon, text or shape; icon-only buttons carry an accessibility label.

#### Icons (Locked)
Stroke 1.5pt, round caps and joins, 0 to 2pt corners, outline at rest and filled in `Palette.ink` when selected. SwiftUI: SF Symbols at weight `.regular`, monochrome rendering; template-rendered SVG assets only for glyphs SF Symbols lacks.
- Sizes as the Built screens draw them: 24pt tab bar icons over their 10pt labels; 20pt icons in icon buttons, centred in a 44x44pt hit area; 18pt icons inside the 32pt feature-row chips; 16pt chevrons in the trailing slot of rows, never a text glyph (no ›, > or →); 24pt anywhere else.
- Glyphs the kit uses (draw each once, same stroke): back, more, notifications (bell), search, chevron right, chevron down, check, plus, info, success, warning, error, inbox (empty state), home, list, heart, user.

#### Fonts to bundle (Locked)
- Plus Jakarta Sans: 300 (Display, UI kit) as `PlusJakartaSans-Light`, 600 (Large title, Title, Title 2, UI kit) as `PlusJakartaSans-SemiBold`.
- Inter: 400 (Body, Callout, Subhead, Footnote, UI kit) as `Inter-Regular`, 500 (Headline, UI kit) as `Inter-Medium`, 600 (Eyebrow, Label, Caption, Tab label, UI kit) as `Inter-SemiBold`.
- Geist Mono: 600 (UI kit) as `GeistMono-SemiBold`.
- System faces, no file to bundle: -apple-system.
- Bundle exactly these weights and no others; the type scale uses no more. SwiftUI: use static TTFs (the Google Fonts download, `static/` folder), add them to the target and list them under `UIAppFonts` (iOS) or `ATSApplicationFontsPath` (macOS). The names above are PostScript names.

#### Signature and product imagery (Guided)
- Embedded product screenshots, app icons and product imagery keep their own colours. They are exempt from the canvas and colour coverage budget and from the accent and primary limits; the studio frame around them (surfaces, captions, borders) follows the brand rules.
- Coverage and whitespace targets apply to the brand's own surfaces, not to product pages that showcase a product's own visual system.

#### Do and don't (Guided)
- Don't: Darken the gradient to make white type legible; move the type off it instead.

#### Theme.swift (Locked)
Add this file to the target as is. Build every view from `Palette`, `Font.brand*`, `Metrics`, `Spacing`, `Motion`, `ThemeButtonStyle`, `themeSurface()`, `themeField()` and `themePage()`; never hard-code a color, size or duration. It covers the pressed, disabled, focus and error states; empty and loading follow the States table.
```swift
// Theme.swift: OpenText, Mesh Gradient. Generated by Looks Good; change the brief, not these values.
import SwiftUI

extension Color {
    init(hex: UInt32, opacity: Double = 1) {
        self.init(.sRGB, red: Double((hex >> 16) & 0xFF) / 255, green: Double((hex >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255, blue: Double(hex & 0xFF) / 255, opacity: opacity)
    }
    /// Adaptive color: follows the system light or dark appearance.
    init(light: UInt32, dark: UInt32, lightOpacity: Double = 1, darkOpacity: Double = 1) {
        #if canImport(UIKit)
        self = Color(UIColor { $0.userInterfaceStyle == .dark ? UIColor(Color(hex: dark, opacity: darkOpacity)) : UIColor(Color(hex: light, opacity: lightOpacity)) })
        #else
        self = Color(NSColor(name: nil) { $0.bestMatch(from: [.darkAqua, .aqua]) == .darkAqua ? NSColor(Color(hex: dark, opacity: darkOpacity)) : NSColor(Color(hex: light, opacity: lightOpacity)) })
        #endif
    }
}

enum Palette {
    static let canvas = Color(light: 0xF5F7F9, dark: 0x1C2126)   // The screen background
    static let ink = Color(light: 0x1C2126, dark: 0xF5F7F9)   // Default text and icons
    static let muted = Color(light: 0x686C70, dark: 0x9EA1A5)   // Secondary text from the palette
    static let line = Color(light: 0xD4D7D9, dark: 0x3D4146)   // Hairlines, dividers, resting input borders
    static let primary = Color(light: 0x98B0CC, dark: 0x98B0CC)   // Main action
    static let accent = Color(light: 0xCACD7E, dark: 0xCACD7E)   // One spark
    static let priTint = Color(light: 0xEAEEF4, dark: 0x2B323A)   // Quiet primary wash: selected background, hover on primary-linked items
    static let secTint = Color(light: 0xF2F1F3, dark: 0x3B3D41)   // Quiet secondary wash: selected row, tonal fills, hover on secondary it
    static let error = Color(light: 0x1C2126, dark: 0xF5F7F9)   // Errors and destructive actions; never a stock red
    static let card = Color(light: 0xFFFFFF, dark: 0x2B3035)   // Card and panel fill (paper); everything else sits on the canvas
    static let kitPrimary = Color(light: 0x0A2540, dark: 0x000000, lightOpacity: 0.06, darkOpacity: 0.16)   // Measured from the UI kit in light and dark

extension Font {
    static let brandDisplay = Font.custom("PlusJakartaSans-Light", size: 34, relativeTo: .largeTitle)
    static let brandEyebrow = Font.custom("Inter-SemiBold", size: 13, relativeTo: .caption)
    static let brandLabel = Font.custom("Inter-SemiBold", size: 13, relativeTo: .caption)
    static let brandLargetitle = Font.custom("PlusJakartaSans-SemiBold", size: 34, relativeTo: .largeTitle)
    static let brandTitle = Font.custom("PlusJakartaSans-SemiBold", size: 28, relativeTo: .title)
    static let brandTitle2 = Font.custom("PlusJakartaSans-SemiBold", size: 22, relativeTo: .title2)
    static let brandHeadline = Font.custom("Inter-Medium", size: 17, relativeTo: .headline)
    static let brandBody = Font.custom("Inter-Regular", size: 17, relativeTo: .body)
    static let brandCallout = Font.custom("Inter-Regular", size: 16, relativeTo: .callout)
    static let brandSubhead = Font.custom("Inter-Regular", size: 15, relativeTo: .subheadline)
    static let brandFootnote = Font.custom("Inter-Regular", size: 13, relativeTo: .footnote)
    static let brandCaption = Font.custom("Inter-SemiBold", size: 12, relativeTo: .caption)
    static let brandTablabel = Font.custom("Inter-SemiBold", size: 10, relativeTo: .caption2)
    // Display and Title: tracking -0.035em. Apply .textCase(.uppercase) and .tracking(...) at the call site.
}

enum Metrics {
    static let radiusControl: CGFloat = 999
    static let radiusField: CGFloat = 12
    static let radiusCard: CGFloat = 20
    static let outline: CGFloat = 1
    static let hit: CGFloat = 44   // minimum tappable size
    static let margin: CGFloat = 20   // side margin of every screen
    static let buttonHeight: CGFloat = 52, fieldHeight: CGFloat = 48, rowHeight: CGFloat = 52, cardPadding: CGFloat = 20
}

enum Spacing {
    static let xs: CGFloat = 4, s: CGFloat = 8, m: CGFloat = 16, l: CGFloat = 24, xl: CGFloat = 32
    static let sm: CGFloat = 12, ml: CGFloat = 20, xxl: CGFloat = 40, xxxl: CGFloat = 48
}

enum Motion {
    static let fast: Double = 0.12, base: Double = 0.18, slow: Double = 0.28
    static let press: Animation = .easeOut(duration: fast)
    static let settle: Animation = .easeOut(duration: base)
}

struct ThemeButtonStyle: ButtonStyle {
    @Environment(\.isEnabled) private var isEnabled
    @Environment(\.accessibilityReduceMotion) private var reduceMotion
    func makeBody(configuration: Configuration) -> some View {
        let shape = RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: Metrics.radiusControl, style: .continuous)
        let down = configuration.isPressed
        return configuration.label
            .font(.brandHeadline)
            .foregroundStyle(isEnabled ? Palette.onPrimary : Palette.muted)
            .padding(.horizontal, Spacing.l)
            .frame(minHeight: Metrics.hit)
            .background(isEnabled ? Palette.primary : Palette.line, in: shape)
            .opacity(down ? 0.85 : 1)
            .animation(reduceMotion ? nil : Motion.press, value: down)
    }
}

struct ThemeSurface: ViewModifier {
    func body(content: Content) -> some View {
        let shape = RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: Metrics.radiusCard, style: .continuous)
        return content
            .padding(Spacing.m)
            .background(Palette.card, in: shape)
            .overlay(shape.strokeBorder(Palette.line, lineWidth: Metrics.outline))
    }
}

struct ThemeField: ViewModifier {
    var focused = false
    var failed = false
    func body(content: Content) -> some View {
        let shape = RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: Metrics.radiusField, style: .continuous)
        return content
            .font(.brandBody)
            .padding(.horizontal, Spacing.m)
            .frame(minHeight: Metrics.hit)
            .background(Palette.canvas, in: shape)
            .overlay(shape.strokeBorder(failed ? Palette.error : Palette.line, lineWidth: failed ? 2 : Metrics.outline))
            .overlay(shape.inset(by: -4).stroke(Palette.primary, lineWidth: focused ? 3 : 0))
            .overlay(shape.inset(by: -6).stroke(Palette.ink, lineWidth: focused ? 1 : 0))
    }
}

extension View {
    func themeSurface() -> some View { modifier(ThemeSurface()) }
    func themeField(focused: Bool = false, failed: Bool = false) -> some View { modifier(ThemeField(focused: focused, failed: failed)) }
    /// Page background and default ink; put it on the root view.
    func themePage() -> some View { background(Palette.canvas.ignoresSafeArea()).foregroundStyle(Palette.ink) }
}
```

#### App icon (Locked)
Full-bleed `#98b0cc` tile with one simple glyph (or the brand initial in the heading face) in `#141414`; flat, no shadow, no gradient. Deliver a 1024x1024 master without transparency or pre-rounded corners (the system masks it), a dark variant on `#1c2126` and a tinted variant (glyph only, one color), and derive every other size from the master.

## Part 2: Design tokens

These are the brand's tokens: use them as given, and use nothing else for colour, type or spacing. The first block is the same file "Save + files" writes; the last block holds the values of every custom property the Part 3 CSS reads, resolved on the kit root, so every `var(--x)` in Part 3 has a value here.

### Theme.swift

```swift
// Theme.swift: OpenText, Mesh Gradient. Generated by Looks Good; change the brief, not these values.
import SwiftUI

extension Color {
    init(hex: UInt32, opacity: Double = 1) {
        self.init(.sRGB, red: Double((hex >> 16) & 0xFF) / 255, green: Double((hex >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255, blue: Double(hex & 0xFF) / 255, opacity: opacity)
    }
    /// Adaptive color: follows the system light or dark appearance.
    init(light: UInt32, dark: UInt32, lightOpacity: Double = 1, darkOpacity: Double = 1) {
        #if canImport(UIKit)
        self = Color(UIColor { $0.userInterfaceStyle == .dark ? UIColor(Color(hex: dark, opacity: darkOpacity)) : UIColor(Color(hex: light, opacity: lightOpacity)) })
        #else
        self = Color(NSColor(name: nil) { $0.bestMatch(from: [.darkAqua, .aqua]) == .darkAqua ? NSColor(Color(hex: dark, opacity: darkOpacity)) : NSColor(Color(hex: light, opacity: lightOpacity)) })
        #endif
    }
}
```

### Resolved custom properties read by the kit CSS

Set these on the kit root (the element carrying class `ab sb-ab`) before the Part 3 CSS applies. The first block is the light look; the second lists only the values that differ in dark, scoped by `data-mode`.

```css
.ab.sb-ab {
  --ab-accent: #cacd7e;
  --ab-canvas: #f5f7f9;
  --ab-cta-sh: 0 1px 1px rgba(10,37,64,.08), 0 6px 14px -4px rgba(50,50,93,.3), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.28);
  --ab-display: 1.2;
  --ab-fill: rgba(28,33,38,0.045);
  --ab-font-b: "Inter", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --ab-font-h: "Plus Jakarta Sans", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --ab-font-label: "Inter", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --ab-font-ui: "Inter", -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --ab-hair: rgba(28,33,38,0.09);
  --ab-hl-scale: 1;
  --ab-hs: 1;
  --ab-ink: #1c2126;
  --ab-line: #d4d7d9;
  --ab-m-hue: #9493d1;
  --ab-mesh-swash: #cacd7e;
  --ab-muted: #686c70;
  --ab-on-accent: #141414;
  --ab-on-primary: #141414;
  --ab-on-secondary: #141414;
  --ab-paper: #ffffff;
  --ab-pri-tint: #eaeef4;
  --ab-primary: #98b0cc;
  --ab-primary-text: #1c2126;
  --ab-ring: rgba(152,176,204,0.3);
  --ab-sec-tint: #f2f1f3;
  --ab-secondary: #e0d1d1;
  --ab-shadow: 0 1px 1px rgba(10,37,64,.06), 0 13px 27px -5px rgba(50,50,93,.25), 0 8px 16px -8px rgba(0,0,0,.3);
  --ab-shadow-lift: 0 1px 1px rgba(10,37,64,.06), 0 30px 60px -12px rgba(50,50,93,.25), 0 18px 36px -18px rgba(0,0,0,.3);
  --ab-tr: 1;
  --k-body: 17px;
  --k-body-lh: 24px;
  --k-btn: 52px;
  --k-callout: 16px;
  --k-callout-lh: 22px;
  --k-caption: 12px;
  --k-caption-lh: 16px;
  --k-cardpad: 20px;
  --k-err: #1c2126;
  --k-err-ico: #1c2126;
  --k-field: 48px;
  --k-footnote: 13px;
  --k-footnote-lh: 18px;
  --k-gap: 28px;
  --k-headline: 17px;
  --k-headline-lh: 22px;
  --k-info: #98b0cc;
  --k-info-ico: #73859a;
  --k-large-title: 34px;
  --k-large-title-lh: 41px;
  --k-margin: 20px;
  --k-muted: color-mix(in srgb, #1c2126 28%, #686c70);
  --k-ok: #e0d1d1;
  --k-ok-ico: #888284;
  --k-pri-on-tint: #5a6979;
  --k-pri-text: #607081;
  --k-r: min(999px, 16px);
  --k-row: 52px;
  --k-subhead: 15px;
  --k-subhead-lh: 20px;
  --k-tab: 83px;
  --k-tab-label: 10px;
  --k-tab-label-lh: 12px;
  --k-tap: 44px;
  --k-title: 28px;
  --k-title-2: 22px;
  --k-title-2-lh: 28px;
  --k-title-lh: 34px;
  --k-ui: Inter, -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --k-warn: #cacd7e;
  --k-warn-ico: #84885b;
  --mg-kit-cta: 0 1px 2px rgba(10, 37, 64, 0.06), 0 6px 16px -8px rgba(50, 50, 93, 0.18), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.24);
  --mg-kit-grad: linear-gradient(140deg, #98b0cc, #9493d1);
  --mg-kit-halo: 0 0 0 4px color-mix(in srgb, #98b0cc 22%, transparent);
  --mg-kit-sel: linear-gradient(90deg, #eaeef4, transparent);
  --mg-kit-sh: 0 1px 2px rgba(10, 37, 64, 0.05), 0 6px 18px -8px rgba(50, 50, 93, 0.16);
  --mg-kit-sh-lg: 0 2px 4px rgba(10, 37, 64, 0.06), 0 18px 40px -12px rgba(50, 50, 93, 0.28);
  --mg-kit-sh-sm: 0 1px 2px rgba(10, 37, 64, 0.06), 0 3px 10px -4px rgba(50, 50, 93, 0.16);
  --sb-card-r: 20px;
  --sb-danger: #1c2126;
  --sb-danger-on: #f5f7f9;
  --sb-danger-text: #1c2126;
  --sb-focus-auto: #1c2126;
  --sb-focus-offset: 3px;
  --sb-focus-w: 2px;
  --sb-r: 999px;
  --sb-ui-ff: Inter, -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --sb-ui-ls: -0.085px;
  --sb-ui-tt: none;
}
```

```css
.ab.sb-ab[data-mode="dark"] {
  --ab-canvas: #1c2126;
  --ab-cta-sh: 0 1px 1px rgba(0,0,0,.4), 0 6px 14px -4px rgba(0,0,0,.5), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.2);
  --ab-fill: rgba(245,247,249,0.07);
  --ab-hair: rgba(245,247,249,0.14);
  --ab-ink: #f5f7f9;
  --ab-line: #3d4146;
  --ab-muted: #a7aaad;
  --ab-paper: #2b3035;
  --ab-pri-tint: #2b323a;
  --ab-primary-text: #98b0cc;
  --ab-sec-tint: #3b3d41;
  --ab-shadow: 0 1px 1px rgba(0,0,0,.4), 0 13px 27px -5px rgba(0,0,0,.55), 0 8px 16px -8px rgba(0,0,0,.6);
  --ab-shadow-lift: 0 1px 1px rgba(0,0,0,.4), 0 30px 60px -12px rgba(0,0,0,.6), 0 18px 36px -18px rgba(0,0,0,.7);
  --k-err: #f5f7f9;
  --k-err-ico: #f5f7f9;
  --k-info-ico: #98b0cc;
  --k-muted: color-mix(in srgb, #f5f7f9 28%, #a7aaad);
  --k-ok-ico: #e0d1d1;
  --k-pri-on-tint: #98b0cc;
  --k-pri-text: #98b0cc;
  --k-warn-ico: #cacd7e;
  --mg-kit-cta: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16), 0 6px 16px -8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.26), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
  --mg-kit-sel: linear-gradient(90deg, #2b323a, transparent);
  --mg-kit-sh: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16), 0 8px 22px -8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.26);
  --mg-kit-sh-lg: 0 2px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16), 0 18px 40px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.26);
  --mg-kit-sh-sm: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16), 0 3px 10px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.22);
  --sb-danger: #f5f7f9;
  --sb-danger-on: #1c2126;
  --sb-danger-text: #f5f7f9;
  --sb-focus-auto: #98b0cc;
}
```

Set per element (in the markup's inline `style` or by a rule inside `kit.css`, not on the root): `--h`, `--k-dv`, `--k-dv-w`, `--k-dv-x`, `--k-inset`, `--k-picked`, `--pr`, `--t`, `--tone`, `--tone-ico`, `--v`, `--wr`.

Optional hooks this style does not set (each `var()` in `kit.css` carries its own fallback, so leave them unset): `--k-arrow-edge`, `--k-callout-edge-w`, `--k-callout-fill`, `--k-callout-r`, `--k-callout-shadow`, `--k-dis-label`, `--k-sheet-r`, `--k-sheet-shadow`, `--sb-focus`.

## Part 3: UI kit source

First comes `kit.css`: every CSS rule that draws the UI kit in this style, in cascade order, grouped by the file it came from. Then each component, with what it is for, its iOS treatment, its states and the reference markup of every state. Port the CSS values exactly; treat the markup as illustrative.

### Kit root

Every selector in `kit.css` is scoped to this root. Rename or fold the scope into your own, but keep the same attributes' meaning (`data-ae` is the style, `data-mode` is light or dark).

```html
<section class="ab sb-ab sb-window sb-kit" data-ae="mesh-gradient" data-mode="light" data-accent-text="low" data-hero="split" data-palette="swatches" data-sections="two-col" data-highlight="custom" data-states="button" data-subcopy="on" data-hcls="geometric" data-bcls="neo-grotesk" data-sb-danger="ink" data-k-field="box">
  <!-- a component's reference markup goes here -->
</section>
```

### kit.css

624 rules from 4 files: `baseline`, `css/aesthetic-board.css`, `css/aesthetics/mesh-gradient.css`, `css/see-built.css`.

```css
/* ===== baseline (from css/styles.css) ===== */
*,
::before,
::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *,
  ::before,
  ::after {
    animation-duration: 0.001ms !important;
    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
    transition-duration: 0.001ms !important;
  }
}

/* ===== css/aesthetic-board.css ===== */
.ab {
  container-type: inline-size;
  container-name: ab;
  position: relative;
  isolation: isolate;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-family: var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b));
  --ab-pad: clamp(20px, 4.4cqw, 44px);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 400;
  line-height: 1.5;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  text-transform: none;
  text-align: left;
  border-radius: 20px;
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  font-synthesis-small-caps: none;
  font-synthesis-style: none;
  font-synthesis-weight: none;
}

.ab *,
.ab ::before,
.ab ::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

:where(.ab) button,
:where(.ab) input {
  font-style: inherit;
  font-variant-caps: inherit;
  font-weight: inherit;
  font-width: inherit;
  font-size: inherit;
  line-height: inherit;
  font-family: inherit;
  font-size-adjust: inherit;
  font-kerning: inherit;
  font-variant-alternates: inherit;
  font-variant-ligatures: inherit;
  font-variant-numeric: inherit;
  font-variant-east-asian: inherit;
  font-variant-position: inherit;
  font-variant-emoji: inherit;
  font-feature-settings: inherit;
  font-optical-sizing: inherit;
  font-variation-settings: inherit;
  color: inherit;
}

.ab h2,
.ab h3,
.ab p,
.ab ul {
  margin: 0px;
  padding: 0px;
}

.ab i {
  font-style: normal;
}

.ab-brand {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  min-width: 0px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  font-size: clamp(15px, 2cqw, 19px);
}

.ab-wordmark {
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ab:not(.ab-slide) .ab-wordmark {
  overflow: visible;
  text-overflow: clip;
  white-space: normal;
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
}

.ab-mark {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  font: 700 14px/1 var(--ab-font-h);
}

.ab-rule {
  height: 1px;
  background: var(--ab-line);
}

.ab-label,
.ab-fieldlabel {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label, var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b)));
}

.ab-labelrow {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: baseline;
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 16px;
}

.ab-label {
  font-size: 11px;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
}

.ab-headline {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
  font-size: calc(clamp(32px, 7.4cqw, 70px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  text-wrap: balance;
  overflow-wrap: normal;
}

.ab[data-ae] .ab-headline:not([data-wrap-last-resort]) {
  overflow-wrap: normal;
}

.ab-hl-glue {
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ab-subcopy {
  max-width: 36em;
  text-wrap: pretty;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: clamp(13.5px, 1.5cqw, 16px);
  line-height: 1.5;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  margin-top: clamp(14px, 2cqw, 22px) !important;
}

.ab-card {
  padding: clamp(18px, 2.8cqw, 26px);
  border-radius: 16px;
}

.ab-seg {
  display: flex;
  gap: 4px;
  padding: 5px;
  border-radius: 12px;
}

.ab-seg-btn {
  flex: 1 1 0%;
  min-height: 36px;
  padding: 8px 6px;
  border: 0px;
  background: none;
  border-radius: 9px;
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  cursor: pointer;
}

.ab-seg-btn[aria-pressed="true"] {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab-card-title {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-h);
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: clamp(21px, 2.7cqw, 27px);
  line-height: 1.12;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  margin: 24px 0px 8px !important;
}

.ab-card-body {
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: 13px;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  margin-bottom: 22px !important;
}

.ab-cta {
  width: 100%;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 13px 16px;
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  font-size: 13.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab-cta-arrow::before {
  content: "↗";
}

.ab-fieldlabel {
  display: block;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
  font-size: 12px;
}

.ab .ab-input {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: auto;
  margin-bottom: 18px;
  padding: 13px 15px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: none;
  color: inherit;
  font-family: inherit;
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: inherit;
  text-transform: none;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  outline: medium;
}

.ab[data-ae] .ab-input:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

.ab-toggle {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 12px;
  min-height: 44px;
  font-size: 13px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.ab-switch {
  appearance: none;
  position: relative;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  margin: 0px;
  width: 50px;
  height: 29px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  cursor: pointer;
  background: var(--ab-line);
  transition: background 0.18s;
}

.ab-switch::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: 4px;
  top: 4px;
  width: 21px;
  height: 21px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-canvas);
  transition: transform 0.18s;
}

.ab-switch:checked {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab-switch:checked::before {
  transform: translateX(21px);
}

.ab-list {
  list-style: none;
}

.ab-row {
  display: grid;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px clamp(10px, 2cqw, 20px);
  padding: 13px 2px;
  grid-template-columns: 2.6ch minmax(0px, 1fr) auto minmax(60px, 150px) 4.5ch;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  font-size: 13px;
}

.ab-row:last-child {
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
}

.ab-row-bar {
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: var(--ab-line);
  overflow: hidden;
}

.ab-row-bar i {
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
  background: var(--ab-secondary);
}

.ab-fit {
  display: inline-block;
  padding: 2px 9px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-line);
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-label, var(--ab-font-ui, var(--ab-font-b)));
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1.5;
  white-space: nowrap;
  letter-spacing: 0px;
  text-transform: none;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab-fit[data-fit="match"] {
  background: var(--ab-sec-tint);
  border-color: transparent;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab-type-apply {
  padding: 10px 16px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-ink);
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: none;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  cursor: pointer;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.ab-type-apply:hover {
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
}

.ab-type-apply:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-ink);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

@container ab (max-width: 600px) {
  .ab-headline {
    font-size: calc(clamp(30px, 10.5cqw, 52px) * var(--ab-hl-scale, 1) * var(--ab-hs, 1) * var(--ab-display, 1));
  }

  .ab-brand {
    flex: 1 1 auto;
  }

  .ab-labelrow {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    row-gap: 4px;
  }

  .ab-labelrow > .ab-label:last-child:not(:first-child) {
    white-space: nowrap;
  }

  .ab-row {
    grid-template-columns: 2.6ch minmax(0px, 1fr) 4.5ch;
    row-gap: 6px;
  }

  .ab-row-bar {
    display: none;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .ab *,
  .ab ::before,
  .ab ::after {
    transition: none !important;
    animation: auto !important;
  }
}

/* ===== css/aesthetics/mesh-gradient.css ===== */
.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] {
  --mg-mono: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  --mg-head: 56px;
  --mg-cut: 10.5cqw;
  border-radius: 20px;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-brand {
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: calc(-0.03em * var(--ab-tr, 1));
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-mark {
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  border-radius: 9px;
  background: var(--ab-ink);
  color: var(--ab-canvas);
  box-shadow: rgba(10, 37, 64, 0.12) 0px 1px 1px, rgba(50, 50, 93, 0.28) 0px 4px 10px -2px;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-headline {
  font-weight: 300;
  line-height: 1.02;
  letter-spacing: calc(-0.035em * var(--ab-tr, 1));
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  text-wrap: balance;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-hl {
  color: inherit;
  background: linear-gradient(var(--ab-mesh-swash), var(--ab-mesh-swash)) 0 92% / 100% 0.16em no-repeat;
  padding: 0px 0.02em 0.06em;
  -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-subcopy {
  max-width: 25em;
  font-family: var(--ab-font-b);
  font-size: clamp(14px, 1.7cqw, 18px);
  line-height: 1.5;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-label {
  font-size: 11px;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-labelrow {
  align-items: flex-start;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-labelrow .ab-label:first-child {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-rule {
  background: var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-fit {
  border-color: var(--ab-hair);
  background: var(--ab-fill);
  font-size: 10.5px;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-fit[data-fit="match"] {
  background: var(--ab-fill);
  border-color: var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-type-apply {
  padding: 10px 18px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: var(--ab-shadow);
  transition: transform 0.15s, box-shadow 0.15s;
  letter-spacing: -0.005em;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-type-apply:hover {
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  transform: translateY(-1px);
  box-shadow: var(--ab-shadow-lift);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-type-apply:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-primary-text);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-card,
.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-list {
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  border-radius: 12px;
  box-shadow: var(--ab-shadow);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-card {
  padding: clamp(18px, 2.8cqw, 24px);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-seg {
  gap: 2px;
  padding: 3px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: var(--ab-fill);
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-seg-btn {
  min-height: 34px;
  border-radius: 7px;
  font-size: 12.5px;
  font-weight: 500;
  transition: background 0.15s, box-shadow 0.15s, color 0.15s;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-seg-btn:hover {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-seg-btn[aria-pressed="true"] {
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
  box-shadow: inset 0 -2px 0 var(--ab-primary), 0 1px 2px var(--ab-hair), 0 3px 8px -2px var(--ab-ring);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-card-title {
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: calc(-0.03em * var(--ab-tr, 1));
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-card-body {
  font-size: 13.5px;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta {
  width: auto;
  display: inline-flex;
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 9px 9px 9px 18px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: -0.005em;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  box-shadow: var(--ab-cta-sh);
  transition: transform 0.15s, box-shadow 0.15s, filter 0.15s;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta:hover {
  filter: brightness(1.06);
  transform: translateY(-1px);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta:active {
  transform: translateY(1px);
  filter: brightness(0.96);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta-arrow {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 26px;
  height: 26px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  transition: transform 0.18s cubic-bezier(0.3, 0.9, 0.4, 1.2);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta-arrow::before {
  content: "→";
  font-size: 14px;
  line-height: 1;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta:hover .ab-cta-arrow {
  transform: translateX(3px);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-fieldlabel {
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 500;
  color: var(--ab-muted);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-input {
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 11px 13px;
  font-size: 14px;
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  box-shadow: inset 0 1px 2px var(--ab-hair);
  transition: border-color 0.15s, box-shadow 0.15s;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-input:focus {
  border-color: var(--ab-primary);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px var(--ab-ring);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-toggle > span {
  font-weight: 500;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-switch {
  width: 38px;
  height: 22px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--ab-hair);
  box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12) 0px 1px 2px inset;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-switch::before {
  left: 2px;
  top: 2px;
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
  background: rgb(255, 255, 255);
  box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.28) 0px 1px 3px;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-switch:checked {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2) 0px 1px 2px inset;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-switch:checked::before {
  transform: translateX(16px);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-list {
  overflow: hidden;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-row {
  grid-template-columns: 2.6ch 28px minmax(0px, 1fr) auto minmax(60px, 140px) 4.5ch;
  padding: 12px 18px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  gap: 4px clamp(10px, 1.8cqw, 16px);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-row:first-child {
  border-top: 0px;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-row:last-child {
  border-bottom: 0px;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-row-bar {
  height: 6px;
  background: var(--ab-fill);
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-row-bar i {
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  border-radius: 3px;
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-seg-btn:focus-visible,
.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta:focus-visible,
.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-switch:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-primary-text);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

@container ab (max-width: 600px) {
  .ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] {
    --mg-cut: 9cqw;
    --mg-head: 52px;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-row {
    grid-template-columns: 28px minmax(0px, 1fr) auto 4.5ch;
    padding: 11px 14px;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta,
  .ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta-arrow,
  .ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-type-apply {
    transition: none;
  }

  .ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta:hover,
  .ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta:active,
  .ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta:hover .ab-cta-arrow,
  .ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-type-apply:hover {
    transform: none;
  }
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-cta {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--ab-primary), var(--ab-m-hue));
}

.ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] .ab-row-bar i {
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--ab-primary), var(--ab-m-hue));
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"].sb-kit {
  --mg-kit-sh: 0 1px 2px rgba(10, 37, 64, 0.05), 0 6px 18px -8px rgba(50, 50, 93, 0.16);
  --mg-kit-sh-sm: 0 1px 2px rgba(10, 37, 64, 0.06), 0 3px 10px -4px rgba(50, 50, 93, 0.16);
  --mg-kit-cta: 0 1px 2px rgba(10, 37, 64, 0.06), 0 6px 16px -8px rgba(50, 50, 93, 0.18), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.24);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"].sb-kit[data-mode="dark"] {
  --mg-kit-sh: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16), 0 8px 22px -8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.26);
  --mg-kit-sh-sm: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16), 0 3px 10px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.22);
  --mg-kit-cta: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16), 0 6px 16px -8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.26), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.16);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"].sb-kit :is(.ab-card, .ab-panel, .ab-list) {
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-sh);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"].sb-kit .ab-type-apply {
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-sh-sm);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .ab-cta {
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-cta);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"].sb-kit .ab-seg-btn[aria-pressed="true"] {
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-sh-sm);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"].sb-kit .ab-hl {
  padding: 0px;
  -webkit-box-decoration-break: slice;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"].sb-kit :is(.ab-headline, .sb-home-h, .sb-k-ltitle) {
  letter-spacing: calc(-0.012em * var(--ab-tr, 1));
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit[data-mode="dark"] .ab-seg {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, #000 24%, var(--ab-paper));
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit[data-mode="dark"] .ab-seg-btn[aria-pressed="true"] {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 22%, var(--ab-paper));
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08), var(--mg-kit-sh-sm);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"].sb-kit .ab-list {
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"].sb-kit .ab-input {
  border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 32%, transparent);
}

.ab.sb-ab[data-ae="mesh-gradient"].sb-kit .sb-k-av {
  background: linear-gradient(140deg, var(--ab-primary), var(--ab-m-hue));
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit {
  --mg-kit-sh-lg: 0 2px 4px rgba(10, 37, 64, 0.06), 0 18px 40px -12px rgba(50, 50, 93, 0.28);
  --mg-kit-grad: linear-gradient(140deg, var(--ab-primary), var(--ab-m-hue));
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit[data-mode="dark"] {
  --mg-kit-sh-lg: 0 2px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.16), 0 18px 40px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.26);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-ph {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: linear-gradient(140deg, var(--ab-pri-tint), var(--ab-fill));
  color: var(--ab-primary-text);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-scrim {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 40%, transparent);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-pop-arrow {
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  border-color: var(--ab-hair);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu {
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-sh-lg);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li {
  gap: 10px;
  border-radius: 8px;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li:hover {
  background: var(--ab-fill);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li[aria-selected="true"] {
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--ab-pri-tint), transparent);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.sb-k-menu-sep {
  min-height: 0px;
  height: 1px;
  margin: 4px 6px;
  padding: 0px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.sb-k-menu-sep:hover {
  background: var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.is-danger,
.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.is-danger .sb-ico {
  color: var(--sb-danger-text, var(--sb-danger));
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cmdmenu li.has-sub.is-open {
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-split-main {
  border-radius: 999px 0px 0px 999px;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-split-more {
  border-radius: 0px 999px 999px 0px;
  border-left: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-on-primary) 35%, transparent);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-fab {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-cta);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-fab:is(.sb-k-pressed, :active) {
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-sh-sm), inset 0 0 0 100vmax color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 8%, transparent);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-fab:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-primary);
  outline-offset: 3px;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-link {
  color: var(--ab-primary-text);
  text-decoration-line: underline;
  text-decoration-thickness: auto;
  text-decoration-style: solid;
  text-decoration-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-primary-text) 40%, transparent);
  text-underline-offset: 3px;
  transition: text-decoration-color 0.15s, color 0.15s;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-link:hover {
  text-decoration-color: currentcolor;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-link:is(:active, .is-pressed, .sb-k-pressed) {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  text-decoration-color: currentcolor;
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-link:is(:focus-visible, .sb-k-focus, .is-focus) {
  border-radius: 4px;
  outline: 2px solid var(--ab-primary);
  outline-offset: 2px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px var(--ab-ring);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-icon {
  padding: 10px 14px;
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-sh-sm);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-icon svg {
  color: var(--ab-primary-text);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-icon span {
  color: var(--k-muted);
  font-weight: 500;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-combo .sb-k-menu {
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-sh-lg);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit mark.sb-k-match {
  padding: 0px 1px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-primary) 16%, transparent);
  color: inherit;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-date-ico {
  color: var(--ab-primary-text);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-listbox {
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-listbox li {
  border-radius: 8px;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-listbox li:hover {
  background: var(--ab-fill);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-listbox li[aria-selected="true"] {
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--ab-pri-tint), transparent);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-stepper {
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-ink) 32%, transparent);
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-sh-sm);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-step {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: none;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-step:hover:not(:disabled) {
  background: var(--ab-fill);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-step:disabled {
  color: var(--ab-muted);
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-step:focus-visible {
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px var(--ab-ring);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-step-val {
  font-weight: 600;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-knob-dial {
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-sh-sm), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.24);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-knob-ind {
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-knob-val {
  font-weight: 600;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cal {
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-sh);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cal-head > span {
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: calc(-0.012em * var(--ab-tr, 1));
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cal-head .sb-k-plain {
  border-radius: 50%;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cal-head .sb-k-plain:hover {
  background: var(--ab-fill);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-cal-dow {
  color: var(--k-muted);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-day {
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: none;
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-day:hover:not(.is-sel):not(.is-out) {
  background: var(--ab-fill);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-day.is-today {
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ab-primary);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-day.is-range {
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: var(--ab-pri-tint);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-day.is-sel {
  background: var(--mg-kit-grad);
  color: var(--ab-on-primary);
  font-weight: 600;
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-sh-sm);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-day.is-start {
  border-radius: 999px 0px 0px 999px;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-day.is-end {
  border-radius: 0px 999px 999px 0px;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-day.is-out {
  color: var(--ab-muted);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-wheel {
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-wheel-item {
  color: var(--ab-muted);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-wheel-item.is-sel {
  color: var(--ab-ink);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-wheel-band {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ab-primary) 10%, transparent);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ab-ring);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-accordion {
  border: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--ab-paper);
  box-shadow: none;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-acc {
  border: 0px;
  border-radius: 0px;
  background: none;
  box-shadow: none;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-acc + .sb-k-acc {
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ab-hair);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-acc-sum:hover {
  background: var(--ab-fill);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-acc[open] > .sb-k-acc-sum {
  color: var(--ab-primary-text);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-acc-chev {
  color: var(--ab-primary-text);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-acc-body {
  color: var(--k-muted);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-car-card {
  border-radius: 12px;
  box-shadow: var(--mg-kit-sh);
}

.ab.sb-ab:is([data-ae="mesh-gradient"], [data-ae="mesh-gradient--classic"]).sb-kit .sb-k-car-card .sb-k-ph {
  border-radius: 8px;
}

.ab.sb-ab.sb-kit[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] {

.ab.sb-ab.sb-kit[data-ae="mesh-gradient"] :is(.ab-cta.sb-k-btn, .ab-type-apply.sb-k-btn2, .ab-type-apply.sb-k-iconbtn).sb-k-pressed {
```
